Houston · four PSSI eigenvector maps

All city maps

Reading the colors: each map shows n × vᵢ, where n is the city’s section count and the right eigenvector sums to one. The arithmetic section mean is 1. The common logarithmic color scale spans 0.25–4 in every panel and city. Values outside that range use the arrow-end colors; observed minima and maxima (rounded for display) and saturation counts are printed on each panel. Zero numerical components use the below-range color and are flagged. Full-precision values remain in the CSVs. These are relative section scores, not citywide eigenvalues or normalized PSSI.

Individual maps

White aloneBlack aloneHispanic · any raceAsian alone

2020 Census populations; locally computed walking and GTFS transit. Walking access and egress have no distance cutoff, while water-chord checks remain. Automatic transfer links connect stops within 400 meters; explicit GTFS links and chained walking transfers are retained, so 400 meters is not a total transfer-walking limit. These approximate paths are not verified street or bridge routes. No Routes API calls were made. Removing water from the map changes only display geometry, not counts, assignments or scores. Hispanic overlaps with the race-alone categories.
